========== MODEL ==========
Now I can see the issue. On line 1876-1887, for Win11 there's a XAML heuristic: if LMB is pressed after RMB within a time window, it's treated as Paste. The bug is: right-click → right-click again → click outside (empty area) = LMB after RMB is being detected as Paste.

========== MODEL ==========
Now I understand the code structure well. Let me trace through the bug scenario:

**Bug scenario**: User right-clicks → context menu appears → right-clicks again (or dismisses by clicking outside) → then clicks on empty area (LMB)

The issue is that `last_rbutton` gets updated to the time of the **second** right-click, so when the user then clicks on empty space (LMB), the time conditions are satisfied:
- `time_since_rbutton` is between 0.18 and 8.0 seconds  
- `time_since_lbutton` < 1.5 seconds
- `_is_explorer_clipboard_client()` returns True

The key insight: when user right-clicks a second time, the context menu from the first right-click closes, and `menu_visible` becomes False. Then the LMB on empty space passes through all the checks.

The fix should track **how many right-clicks** happened, or more specifically, ensure that when the second RMB closes a menu (by clicking elsewhere / right-clicking again), the `last_rbutton` timestamp being close doesn't trigger paste. 

Let me look at where `last_rbutton` is assigned to understand how the mouse hook works:

========== MODEL ==========
Now I can see the full picture. The mouse poll loop in `filecopyhost.py` at line 1691 updates `_agent_last_rbutton_time = time.time()` every time RMB is held down. 

**Bug scenario**:
1. User RMB → context menu opens → `_agent_last_rbutton_time` = T1
2. User RMB again (to dismiss or on another spot) → context menu closes, `_agent_last_rbutton_time` = T2 (recent!)  
3. User LMB on empty area → `_agent_last_lbutton_time` = T3 (T3 > T2, recent)
4. At this point `time_since_rbutton` is small (< 8s), `time_since_lbutton` is small → heuristics in `check_is_menu_query` and `render_format`'s `lmb_after_rmb` can fire, treating it as a Paste

**The fix**: The key insight is that for a "Paste via context menu", the LMB must happen **after a context menu was opened** by the RMB — not just after any RMB. When the user right-clicks a second time without clicking Paste, the old menu is dismissed and the new right-click opens a fresh menu. If they then click somewhere NOT on the menu, it's not a Paste.

Looking at the code more carefully:

- Line 1692: When RMB is detected, `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False` and `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""` are reset
- The problem is that after second RMB dismisses the context menu and the user clicks on empty space (not the menu), `lmb_on_menu=False` and `menu_item_kind=""` (empty, not "not_paste")

Looking at `check_is_menu_query` lines 1876-1887:
```python
if win11 and (
    lmb_down
    and not rmb_down
    and not recent_dblclick
    and menu_item_kind != "not_paste"
    and time_since_rbutton > 0.18
    and time_since_rbutton < 8.0
    and time_since_lbutton < 1.5
    and _is_explorer_clipboard_client()
):
```

This fires even when `lmb_on_menu=False`! The issue is that for Win11, just LMB after RMB + Explorer clipboard client is being treated as Paste. But if the user is clicking on empty space (not the paste menu item), it shouldn't count.

Also look at line 2523-2535 in `render_format` (`lmb_after_rmb`):
```python
lmb_after_rmb = bool(
    last_r
    and last_l
    and last_l > last_r
    and 0.12 < age_r < 8.0
    and age_l < 3.0
    and kind != "not_paste"
    and (
        bool(getattr(self, "_lmb_hit_context_menu", False))
        or kind == "paste"
        or lmb
        or _is_explorer_clipboard_client()  # <-- This catches too many cases!
    )
)
```

The `_is_explorer_clipboard_client()` at the end makes this true even when the user clicks on empty space (not on the menu, not near a paste item).

Now, the key question: **what's the reliable way to detect that clicking on empty space after second RMB should NOT be Paste?**

The most reliable signal is: if the user right-clicks a **second time** without clicking any Paste item, the `_lmb_hit_context_menu` would be `False` and `_lmb_menu_item_kind` would be `""`. After the second RMB, when the user clicks on empty space:
- `_lmb_hit_context_menu = False` (no LMB on menu detected) 
- `menu_item_kind = ""` (not "paste", not "not_paste")
- `menu_visible = False` (menu has been dismissed)

The problem: the `_is_explorer_clipboard_client()` fallback in `lmb_after_rmb` and the Win11 XAML heuristic in `check_is_menu_query` are too broad.

**The real fix**: Track whether there was a context menu that was properly opened and then had a Paste item clicked. We need to track "last_menu_dismiss_time" — when the context menu was dismissed by clicking outside (not on Paste). If the context menu was dismissed (not via Paste click) and the user now LMBs, it should NOT be treated as Paste.

But wait — let me re-read the conditions more carefully:

In `check_is_menu_query` at line 1876-1887, the condition requires:
- `lmb_down` (LMB still being held at the time of this check)
- `_is_explorer_clipboard_client()` 

But when the user clicks on empty space in Explorer, `_is_explorer_clipboard_client()` returns True because Explorer IS the active clipboard client. That's the false positive.

Let me look at what `_is_explorer_clipboard_client` checks:

========== MODEL ==========
Now I see the key mechanism:

- `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` checks at line 1502-1525: it requires `menu_open or menu_just_closed`, `near_rmb`, and `0.12 < age_r < 8.0`
- At line 1477: `rpos = _agent_rmb_cursor_pos` which was set at the **second** right-click position

**The bug**: When user right-clicks a second time on empty space (dismissing the first menu):
1. Second RMB → `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos` = new position, `_agent_last_rbutton_time` = T2
2. Menu closes after second RMB
3. `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` was set while the first menu was open, so `menu_just_closed` could be True
4. User clicks LMB on empty area near the second RMB position → `near_rmb = True`, `menu_just_closed = True` → **triggers false Paste**

Wait, but at line 1492-1496:
```python
menu_just_closed = bool(
    _agent_context_menu_seen_at
    and (now - _agent_context_menu_seen_at) < 0.7
    and not menu_open
)
```

This uses `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` which is set at line 1491 when `menu_open = True`. So if user clicks outside the menu (first click, not second RMB), it would set `menu_just_closed = True` briefly.

Let me think about the actual flow again:

**Exact scenario**: 
1. RMB (first) → context menu appears → `_agent_last_rbutton_time` = T1, `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos` = pos1
2. RMB (second) → context menu dismisses → `_agent_last_rbutton_time` = T2, `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos` = pos2, `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False`, `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""`
3. LMB click on empty space near pos2 → `near_rmb = True`, `menu_just_closed` might be True (if step 2 RMB recently saw menu open)

Actually wait. The second **RMB** itself could be what the poll sees as `menu_open = True` at the moment of second click... Let me re-read: at line 1690 of the mouse poll, when RMB is detected, `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False` and `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""` are reset. But what about `_agent_context_menu_seen_at`?

`_agent_context_menu_seen_at` is set inside `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste()` which is called only when LMB is pressed (not during RMB). So during the second RMB detection, `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` is NOT cleared.

So the flow is:
1. RMB (first) → menu opens → LMB poll still runs → if LMB is pressed anywhere near the menu, `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` gets updated  
2. User doesn't click LMB (stays RMB for the second click)
3. RMB (second) → `_agent_last_rbutton_time = T2`, `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos = pos2`, but `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` still has a value from any previous time
4. User then clicks LMB on empty space → `menu_just_closed = True` (if T2 to now is < 0.7s), `near_rmb = True` → triggers "Paste"

Let me look at when `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` is set and what it tracks:

Actually, re-reading line 1490: `if menu_open: _agent_context_menu_seen_at = now` — this is inside `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste`. The menu was opened after the second RMB. When the second RMB occurs, Windows opens a new context menu briefly... actually no, the second RMB typically dismisses the first menu and then opens a new menu.

Wait, I need to re-read the scenario more carefully: "chuột phải rồi bấm chuột phải rồi bấm ra ngoài khoảng trống" = right-click → right-click again → click outside on empty space.

So: RMB1 → menu opens → RMB2 (clicking somewhere, could be on the menu or outside) → either a new menu opens or old menu closes → LMB on empty space.

The issue is clear now. The poll loop's RMB detection at line 1690 just checks `GetAsyncKeyState(0x02) & 0x8000` — it runs every poll cycle. So during RMB2 being held:
- `_agent_last_rbutton_time = T2` (recent)
- `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos = pos2` 
- `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False`
- `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""`

Then when user LMBs on empty space, `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` is called:
- `pend = True` (there's pending offer)
- `age_r = now - T2` (small, < 8s)
- `menu_open` = might be True if new menu opened from RMB2, or False if it dismissed
- `near_rmb` = might be True if click is near pos2

For `menu_just_closed` to be True, `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` needs to have been set recently. This would have been set when the previous LMB check saw `menu_open = True`.

Actually, the bug might be even simpler. Let me think again:

1. RMB1 → poll detects RMB, sets `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos = pos1`, T1
2. Context menu opens
3. User does RMB2 (on empty area) → poll detects RMB, sets `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos = pos2`, T2
4. RMB2 dismisses menu (and possibly opens a new one at pos2)
5. User then LMBs on empty space. 

If RMB2 opened a **new** context menu that's still open when LMB fires, OR if RMB2 just dismissed the menu and `menu_just_closed = True`:
- `near_rmb = True` (LMB is near pos2)
- `pend = True`
- `0.12 < age_r < 8.0` 
- `menu_open or menu_just_closed = True`
→ Returns True → false Paste!

**The real fix**:

The problem is that clicking on empty space (outside the menu) to dismiss it should not be treated as "Paste". The current code can't distinguish between:
- "LMB near RMB position, with a recently-visible context menu" → actually clicking a menu item
- "LMB near RMB position, with a recently-dismissed menu (dismissed by clicking elsewhere)" → clicking on empty space

The key missing piece: we need to know if the LMB was actually ON the context menu or NOT. The `hit_menu` check at line 1438 handles the "cursor is over context menu" case. 

But for the case where the menu was dismissed by the second RMB (not by an LMB), the user might be clicking on empty space. After the second RMB:
- The first menu closes 
- A second menu appears (or no menu if RMB was on empty area)
- User dismisses second menu by clicking away with LMB

The fix should be: **after a second RMB occurs (while a menu is/was open), the paste trigger timer should be reset**, i.e., `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos = None` OR `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` should be cleared when a new RMB opens a new context menu.

Actually, looking again at the code flow: when `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` is called while a **new** menu (from RMB2) is open, and the LMB is clicking on empty space (dismissing this menu), the check at line 1438 `hit_menu = _cursor_over_context_menu()` returns False. Then we fall to line 1502:

```python
if (
    pend
    and 0.12 < age_r < 8.0
    and not recent_dbl
    and near_rmb
    and not _agent_rmb_is_taskbar
    and (menu_open or menu_just_closed)
):
```

This fires! Because:
- The LMB on empty space CLOSES the menu → but at the time `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` is called (during LMB down), `menu_open` might still be True OR `menu_just_closed` might be True

Actually wait - the poll detects LMB via `GetAsyncKeyState(0x01) & 0x8000`. At the moment the key is detected down, the poll calls `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste`. The context menu is still open at that instant (the LMB is just going down). So `menu_open = True` when the user clicks on empty space to dismiss the menu! **This is the bug.**

The context menu checks `menu_open` to see if there's a context menu visible when LMB fires. But if LMB fires to **dismiss** the context menu (clicking outside), `menu_open` is still True at poll time!

So the fix is: we need to check whether the LMB click is WITHIN the menu's visual bounds (not just "cursor over context menu class" — which might fail for Win11 XAML). For clicking on empty space outside the menu, the cursor is NOT over the menu window.

Looking again at line 1438: `hit_menu = _cursor_over_context_menu()` — this should return False when user clicks outside the menu. But the fallback check at 1502 doesn't require `hit_menu`. It just requires `near_rmb` and `(menu_open or menu_just_closed)`.

The fix: In the fallback block at line 1502, we need to add a check that the click is **not clearly on empty space outside the menu**. If `not hit_menu` and the menu is currently open (`menu_open = True`), the click is likely dismissing the menu (clicking outside), NOT selecting Paste.

Wait but the comment says "Win11 XAML: hit-test menu hay fail" — so `_cursor_over_context_menu()` can fail on Win11. That's why the near_rmb fallback exists.

Let me look at `_cursor_over_context_menu`:

========== MODEL ==========
Now I understand the full picture. `_cursor_over_context_menu()` uses `WindowFromPoint` → this returns the window the cursor is over. If the cursor is NOT over the context menu (e.g., clicking on empty Explorer space), this returns False.

So in `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` at line 1438, when user clicks on empty space:
- `hit_menu = False` (correct, cursor is not over menu)

Then we get to the fallback block at line 1502. Here the bug is: if a context menu is open AND the user clicks on empty space (not over the menu), this code incorrectly considers it as "Paste".

**The critical condition is**: if `not hit_menu` AND `menu_open = True`, it means the user is clicking OUTSIDE the menu (to dismiss it), NOT clicking on a Paste item. This should be `return False`.

But the code at line 1502 allows `menu_open = True` as a valid condition for detecting Paste. This is the bug!

Let me look at the comment: the near_rmb fallback is designed for "Win11 XAML: hit-test menu hay fail". This means on Win11, `_cursor_over_context_menu()` might return False even when the cursor IS over the XAML context menu. So the near_rmb fallback catches these cases.

But the problem is: it also catches the case where cursor is NOT over the menu (user clicking outside). For Win11 XAML menus, the hit-test might fail even when cursor is over the menu... but if cursor is over an XAML menu item and hit-test fails, the cursor must be visually on the menu but WindowFromPoint returns something else.

**Better approach**: When `not hit_menu` and `menu_open = True`, we should check: is the cursor position within the bounding rect of the context menu window? If not, the user is clicking outside to dismiss.

But actually, there's an even simpler and more correct fix: **Track whether the current RMB opened a new context menu.** If `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos` was set by the SECOND right-click (which dismissed a previous menu), and now the user LMBs on empty space to dismiss the second menu, the `_agent_context_menu_seen_at` was last updated during the SECOND menu's lifetime.

The simplest fix: **Add a check that verifies `_cursor_over_context_menu()` OR the cursor is within the bounds of the visible context menu window.** If cursor is definitely outside the menu, don't fire Paste.

Let me look for `_context_menu_rect` or similar:

**Root cause**: In `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` (filecopyhost.py lines 1502-1525), when `hit_menu = False` (cursor NOT on context menu), it still triggers Paste if:
- `menu_open = True` (menu is visible but cursor is outside it)
- `near_rmb = True`

This is wrong: if the menu is open and cursor is NOT on it, the user is clicking outside to dismiss it — NOT selecting Paste.

**The fix**: Add a condition that if `not hit_menu` and `menu_open = True`, we should check if cursor is within the menu's bounding rect. If cursor is outside the menu rect, return False (user is dismissing the menu by clicking outside).

But wait — on Win11 XAML, `_cursor_over_context_menu()` might return False even when cursor IS on the menu. So we can't just do `if not hit_menu and menu_open: return False`.

Instead, we should check if cursor is within the menu's visual bounds via `GetWindowRect`. Let me look for an existing function that does this:

Looking at `_xaml_click_is_menu_body` and `_xaml_click_paste_position` — these check if cursor is within menu bounds. I can use `GetWindowRect` of the menu HWND.

========== MODEL ==========
Now I have a complete understanding. Let me design the minimal fix.

**The bug**: In `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` (filecopyhost.py), the near-RMB fallback block (lines 1502-1525) fires even when:
- The cursor is NOT on the context menu (`hit_menu = False`)  
- The context menu IS open (`menu_open = True`)

This combination means: user is clicking OUTSIDE the visible context menu to dismiss it, NOT clicking Paste. This is the "right-click → right-click → click outside" scenario.

**Fix**: If `not hit_menu` and `menu_open = True`, we need to verify the cursor is within the context menu's bounding rect. If cursor is outside the menu rect, it's a dismiss click, not a Paste click.

Let me create a helper function to check if cursor is within ANY visible context menu rect:

========== MODEL ==========
Now I have a clear picture of the architecture. Let me think about the cleanest fix.

**The exact bug chain**:

Scenario: RMB1 → RMB2 (dismissing menu 1, opening menu 2) → LMB on empty space (dismissing menu 2)

In `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` at LMB time:
- `hit_menu = False` (cursor is on empty space, not on any menu window — because `WindowFromPoint` returns Explorer window or desktop)
- `near_rmb = True` (click is near pos2 of RMB2, which could be same area)
- `menu_open = True` (menu 2 is still visible when LMB first goes down)
- `pend = True`
- `0.12 < age_r < 8.0`
→ Falls into the block at line 1502 → returns True → false Paste!

**Key insight**: `hit_menu = False` means `WindowFromPoint` at cursor position is NOT a context menu class. If `menu_open = True` AND `hit_menu = False`, it means:
- Either the user is clicking outside the menu (to dismiss it) — this is the bug case
- OR the XAML hit-test failed (menu is XAML, cursor is visually on it but WindowFromPoint returns something else)

For the XAML case, the cursor would be near the XAML menu's rect. For the "clicking outside" case, the cursor would be OUTSIDE the menu's rect.

So the fix is: **add a function `_cursor_within_any_context_menu_rect()` and if it returns False when `not hit_menu and menu_open`, treat it as dismiss click (not Paste)**.

Let me also look at the existing function `_visible_context_menu_hwnd` to see if it already exists:

**The targeted fix** I'll implement:

Add a helper `_cursor_within_any_visible_menu_rect()` that:
1. Uses `EnumWindows` to find context menu windows

- If `not hit_menu` and `menu_open`, add check: `_cursor_within_any_visible_menu_rect()` must be True for it to count as Paste
- If cursor is outside all menu rects, return False (user is clicking outside to dismiss)

+def _cursor_within_any_visible_menu_rect(cx, cy):
+    """Kiểm tra tọa độ (cx, cy) có nằm trong vùng hình chữ nhật của bất kỳ cửa sổ context menu
+    nào đang hiển thị không.  Dùng để phân biệt "click vào menu XAML (WindowFromPoint lỗi)"
+    vs "click ra ngoài menu để đóng nó" khi hit_menu = False nhưng menu_open = True."""
+    user32 = ctypes.windll.user32
+
+    class RECT(ctypes.Structure):
+        _fields_ = [
+            ("left", ctypes.c_long), ("top", ctypes.c_long),
+            ("right", ctypes.c_long), ("bottom", ctypes.c_long),
+        ]
+
+    menu_hwnds = []
+
+    def _enum_cb(hwnd, _lp):
+        try:
+            if _hwnd_looks_like_popup_menu(hwnd):
+                menu_hwnds.append(hwnd)
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+        return 1
+
+    try:
+        cb = _WNDENUMPROC(_enum_cb)
+        user32.EnumWindows(cb, 0)
+    except Exception:
+        pass
+
+    rc = RECT()
+    for hwnd in menu_hwnds:
+        try:
+            if user32.GetWindowRect(ctypes.c_void_p(hwnd), ctypes.byref(rc)):
+                if rc.left <= cx <= rc.right and rc.top <= cy <= rc.bottom:
+                    return True
+        except Exception:
+            pass
+    return False
+
+

     pt = wintypes.POINT()
     if not ctypes.windll.user32.GetCursorPos(ctypes.byref(pt)):

Now let me also check the `check_is_menu_query` in `filecopyclient.py` to see if there's a similar bug path there for the "RMB→RMB→LMB outside" scenario. Looking at lines 1876-1887:

```python
if win11 and (
    lmb_down
    and not rmb_down
    and not recent_dblclick
    and menu_item_kind != "not_paste"
    and time_since_rbutton > 0.18
    and time_since_rbutton < 8.0
    and time_since_lbutton < 1.5
    and _is_explorer_clipboard_client()
):
```

This is in `check_is_menu_query` and fires when Win11 detects LMB after RMB with Explorer as clipboard client. But now with the fix in `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste`, `menu_item_kind` will be `""` (not "paste") when the dismiss click fires. The condition `menu_item_kind != "not_paste"` would still pass (since `""` != `"not_paste"`).

However, in `check_is_menu_query`, this path is for the `WM_RENDERFORMAT` handler — which fires when Explorer requests clipboard data. The `render_format` in `filecopyclient.py` is the main gate. The `check_is_menu_query` is its first filter. 

For the false Paste scenario, after my fix, `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False` and `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""`, which means `lmb_on_menu = False` when `check_is_menu_query` is called. However the Win11 XAML heuristic at line 1876 doesn't check `lmb_on_menu`!

========== MODEL ==========
Now I can see the full gate. The host agent's `WM_RENDERFORMAT` handler uses:
1. `check_is_menu_query()` → passes `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu` as `lmb_on_menu`
2. `recent_paste_click` → uses `_agent_paste_gesture_id > _agent_consumed_gesture_id`

The key gate is `recent_paste_click` — this is only true if `_agent_paste_gesture_id > _agent_consumed_gesture_id`, which is set by `_note_paste_gesture()`. My fix in `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` returns False (not calling `_note_paste_gesture()`), so `_agent_paste_gesture_id` is NOT incremented for the false Paste case. 

But the `check_is_menu_query()` Win11 XAML heuristic (line 1876-1887 in filecopyclient.py) could still return False (treating as Paste). Let me trace through:

- `_agent_lmb_hit_context_menu = False` → `lmb_on_menu = False`
- `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = ""` → `menu_item_kind = ""`
- `_agent_paste_gesture_id` is NOT incremented → `recent_paste_click = False`

- Line 1876-1887: Win11 XAML heuristic — `lmb_down=True` (user clicking), `menu_item_kind="" != "not_paste"`, `time_since_rbutton > 0.18`, etc. → **could still return False = Paste!**

But in the host agent handler, after `check_is_menu_query` returns False (thinks it's Paste), it then checks `recent_paste_click`:

- If `is_menu == "MENU"`: checks `recent_paste_click or recent_ctrl`
- If `is_menu == "BACKGROUND"`: checks `recent_paste_click or recent_ctrl and is_exp_target...`
- If `is_menu == False` (thinks it's Paste): continues to paste logic

---

## Summary

**Bug**: RMB → RMB again → LMB on empty space was being misidentified as "Paste file".

**Root cause**: In `_agent_lmb_is_context_paste` (`filecopyhost.py`), the near-RMB fallback block fires when:
- A context menu is open/recently visible (`menu_open = True`)  
- Cursor is near the RMB position (`near_rmb = True`)
- But **cursor is NOT detected over the menu** via `WindowFromPoint` (`hit_menu = False`)

When `menu_open = True` and `hit_menu = False`, this means either:
- ✅ Win11 XAML hit-test failed (cursor IS on menu, just not detectable via `WindowFromPoint`)
- ❌ User is clicking **outside** the menu on empty space to dismiss it (the bug case)

**Fix**: Added a two-part change:

1. **[`filecopyclient.py`]** — New helper `_cursor_within_any_visible_menu_rect(cx, cy)`: enumerates all visible context menu windows via `EnumWindows` + `GetWindowRect`, returning True only if the cursor is inside any of their bounding rects.

2. **[`filecopyhost.py`]** — Before the near-RMB paste block, added a guard: if `menu_open and not hit_menu`, call the new helper. If cursor is outside all menu rects → set `_agent_lmb_menu_item_kind = "not_paste"` and return False. This "not_paste" value propagates correctly through all downstream heuristics (`check_is_menu_query` Win11 XAML path, `lmb_after_rmb`, `fresh_input`) so none of them trigger a false Paste.

33:         # log in real time
34:         print(line, end="")
35:         try:
36:             with open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
37:                 f.write(line)
38:         except:
39:             pass
40:     p.wait()
41:     if p.returncode != 0:
42:         log(f"Command failed with exit code {p.returncode}")
43:         return False
44:     return True
45: 
46: def main():
47:     if os.path.exists(log_path):

129:     for file_name in files_to_copy:
130:         src = os.path.join(workspace_dir, file_name)
131:         dst = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, file_name)

133:             try:
134:                 shutil.copy2(src, dst)
135:                 log(f"Copied {file_name} to app.dist")
136:             except Exception as e:

144:             if os.path.exists(lang_dst):
145:                 shutil.rmtree(lang_dst)

214: def merge_full_service(app_dist_dir):
215:     """Ghép RemoteDesktopService.exe + deps thiếu vào app.dist (giống slim merge của Host)."""
216:     service_dist_dir = os.path.join(workspace_dir, "dist_nuitka_service", "windows_service_loop.dist")
217:     if not os.path.exists(service_dist_dir):
218:         return False
219:     svc_src = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, "windows_service_loop.exe")
220:     svc_dst = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, "RemoteDesktopService.exe")
221:     if not os.path.exists(svc_src):
222:         log("ERROR: windows_service_loop.exe missing in service dist.")
223:         return False
224:     log("Merging RemoteDesktopService.exe (slim — only missing runtime files)...")
225:     shutil.copy2(svc_src, svc_dst)
226:     for item in os.listdir(service_dist_dir):
227:         if item.lower() == "windows_service_loop.exe":
228:             continue
229:         s = os.path.join(service_dist_dir, item)
230:         d = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, item)
231:         if os.path.exists(d):
232:             continue
233:         try:
234:             if os.path.isdir(s):
235:                 shutil.copytree(s, d)
236:             else:
237:                 shutil.copy2(s, d)
238:         except Exception as e:
239:             log(f"Warning: Failed to merge service file {item}: {e}")
240:     try_authenticode_sign(svc_dst)
241:     log("Added RemoteDesktopService.exe")
242:     return True

245: def log_dist_size(app_dist_dir, label):
246:     try:
247:         total = 0
248:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(app_dist_dir):
249:             for name in files:
250:                 try:
251:                     total += os.path.getsize(os.path.join(root, name))
252:                 except Exception:
253:                     pass
254:         log(f"{label}: {total / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB")
255:     except Exception as e:
256:         log(f"Could not measure {label}: {e}")
257: 
258: 
259: def log_file_size(path, label):
260:     try:
261:         if os.path.exists(path):
262:             log(f"{label}: {os.path.getsize(path) / (1024 * 1024):.1f} MB ({path})")
263:     except Exception as e:
264:         log(f"Could not measure {label}: {e}")
265:

267: def shorten_openblas_dll(app_dist_dir):
268:     """Đổi tên DLL OpenBLAS do NumPy đóng gói (tên hash dài) thành libopenblas.dll và vá import trong .pyd."""
269:     import re
270:     short_name = "libopenblas.dll"
271:     long_path = None
272:     long_name = None
273:     try:
274:         names = os.listdir(app_dist_dir)
275:     except Exception as e:
276:         log(f"Không đọc được {app_dist_dir}: {e}")
277:         return
278:     for name in names:
279:         if re.match(r"libopenblas\..+\.dll$", name, re.I) and name.lower() != short_name:
280:             long_path = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, name)
281:             long_name = name
282:             break
283:     if not long_path:
284:         log("No hashed libopenblas DLL found to rename.")
285:         return
286:     old_bytes = long_name.encode("ascii")
287:     short_bytes = short_name.encode("ascii")
288:     if len(short_bytes) >= len(old_bytes):
289:         log(f"Short name {short_name} is not shorter; skip.")
290:         return
291:     padded = short_bytes + b"\x00" * (len(old_bytes) - len(short_bytes))
292:     patched = 0
293:     for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(app_dist_dir):
294:         for f in files:
295:             if not f.lower().endswith((".dll", ".pyd", ".exe")):
296:                 continue
297:             path = os.path.join(root, f)
298:             try:
299:                 with open(path, "rb") as fh:
300:                     data = fh.read()
301:             except Exception:
302:                 continue
303:             if old_bytes not in data:
304:                 continue
305:             data = data.replace(old_bytes, padded)
306:             with open(path, "wb") as fh:
307:                 fh.write(data)
308:             patched += 1
309:             log(f"Patched OpenBLAS import in {os.path.relpath(path, app_dist_dir)}")
310:     dest = os.path.join(app_dist_dir, short_name)
311:     if os.path.abspath(dest) != os.path.abspath(long_path):
312:         if os.path.exists(dest):
313:             try:
314:                 os.remove(dest)
315:             except Exception as e:
316:                 log(f"Could not remove old {short_name}: {e}")
317:                 return
318:         os.rename(long_path, dest)
319:     log(f"Renamed {long_name} -> {short_name} (patched {patched} files).")
320: 
321: def try_authenticode_sign(exe_path):
322:     """Ký Authenticode nếu máy có signtool + chứng chỉ code signing (cách đúng để giảm Defender ML)."""
323:     if not os.path.exists(exe_path):
324:         return
325:     signtool = None
326:     kit = os.path.join(os.environ.get("ProgramFiles(x86)", r"C:\Program Files (x86)"), "Windows Kits", "10", "bin")
327:     if os.path.isdir(kit):
328:         for root, _dirs, files in os.walk(kit):
329:             if "signtool.exe" in files and os.path.basename(root).lower() == "x64":
330:                 signtool = os.path.join(root, "signtool.exe")
331:     if not signtool:
332:         log("Không tìm thấy signtool.exe — bỏ qua ký Authenticode. Defender ML có thể vẫn cảnh báo installer chưa ký.")
333:         log("Cách xử lý: chứng chỉ code signing, hoặc gửi file lên https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/wdsi/filesubmission")
334:         return
335:     cmd = f'"{signtool}" sign /a /fd SHA256 /tr http://timestamp.digicert.com /td SHA256 "{exe_path}"'
336:     if run_cmd(cmd):
337:         log(f"Đã ký Authenticode: {exe_path}")
338:     else:
339:         log("Không ký được (chưa có chứng chỉ code signing). Gửi Microsoft WDSI nếu Defender cảnh báo.")
340: 
341: if __name__ == "__main__":
342:     main()
343:

========== MODEL ==========
Now I have enough to understand both issues fully:

**Bug 1 (still happening, less frequent)**: The `menu_just_closed` path bypasses my fix. When:
- LMB is polled AFTER the menu has closed (timing race with 50ms poll interval)
- `menu_open = False`, `menu_just_closed = True`
- My guard `if menu_open and not hit_menu` doesn't fire

**Bug 2 (old clipboard after disconnect)**: When session ends/disconnects:
- The pipe breaks (`error 109`) but **`_pending_info` is NOT cleared**
- `_agent_rmb_cursor_pos` is also NOT reset
- `_agent_last_rbutton_time` is NOT reset
- User continues to use their machine, RMBs trigger the old RMB state
- After 2-3 RMB+LMB-outside clicks, enough conditions accumulate to fire paste

**Fixes needed:**

1. **Bug 1**: Also guard the `menu_just_closed` path — track `_agent_lmb_dismiss_click_at`. When we detect a dismiss click (cursor outside menu, menu was open), record a timestamp. Then when `menu_just_closed = True`, check if a recent dismiss was recorded.

2. **Bug 2**: On pipe disconnect (`error 109`), clear `_pending_info` and reset the RMB-related gesture flags.

## Tóm tắt sửa lỗi

### Bug 1: RMB → RMB → click ngoài vẫn bị (ít hơn trước)

**Nguyên nhân**: Fix trước chỉ bắt trường hợp `menu_open = True` khi poll LMB. Nếu poll chạy chậm hơn 1 chút (menu đã đóng trước khi poll detect LMB), `menu_open = False` nhưng `menu_just_closed = True` (trong 0.7s), lúc đó fix cũ không kích hoạt.

**Fix thêm**:
- Khi detect **dismiss click** (`menu_open=True` + cursor ngoài menu rect), ghi timestamp vào `_agent_lmb_dismiss_at`
- Thêm guard thứ hai: nếu `menu_just_closed = True` và có dismiss gần đây (< 1s), cũng return `"not_paste"` → chặn Paste nhầm
- Mỗi lần RMB mới reset `_agent_lmb_dismiss_at = 0.0` để không ảnh hưởng lần Paste tiếp theo

### Bug 2: Kết thúc phiên remote vẫn còn clipboard cũ

**Nguyên nhân**: Khi pipe ngắt (error 109 = pipe broken = peer disconnect/session end), code chỉ `break` để reconnect mà **không xóa `_pending_info`**. Clipboard offer từ phiên cũ tồn tại mãi → RMB vài lần sau đó dễ trigger paste dialog với file cũ.

**Fix**: Khi pipe disconnect (error 109), thêm:
```python
if _pending_info:
    _pending_info.clear()  # Xóa offer cũ
_clear_agent_paste_gesture_flags(0.0)  # Reset trạng thái RMB/gesture
```

1096: def _classify_context_menu_item(name):
1097:     """'paste' | 'not_paste' | 'unknown'."""
1098:     raw = (name or "").replace("&", "").replace("…", "").replace("...", "")
1099:     n = " ".join(raw.strip().lower().split())
1100:     if not n:
1101:         return "unknown"
1102:     compact = n.replace(" ", "").replace("+", "")
1103:     if "ctrlv" in compact:
1104:         return "paste"
1105:     first = n.split("\t", 1)[0].strip()
1106:     for tok in _PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1107:         if first == tok or first.startswith(tok + " ") or tok in first.split():
1108:             return "paste"
1109:     for tok in _NOT_PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1110:         if tok in n or tok.replace(" ", "") in compact:
1111:             return "not_paste"
1112:     return "unknown"

1872:     def _write_conflict_query_pipe(paste_id, conflicts, has_multiple):
1873:         """Gửi CONFLICT_QUERY lên host → client hiện ClassicCopyDialog."""
1874:         import win32file, json
1875:         pipe_name = r"\\.\pipe\RemoteDesktopClipboardUpPipe"
1876:         try:
1877:             import win32pipe
1878:             try:
1879:                 win32pipe.WaitNamedPipe(pipe_name, 5000)
1880:             except Exception:
1881:                 pass
1882:             pipe_handle = win32file.CreateFile(
1883:                 pipe_name,
1884:                 win32file.GENERIC_WRITE, 0, None,
1885:                 win32file.OPEN_EXISTING, 0, None
1886:             )
1887:             items = []
1888:             for filename, source_info, dest_info, dest_path in conflicts or []:
1889:                 items.append({
1890:                     "filename": filename,
1891:                     "source_info": source_info or {},
1892:                     "dest_info": dest_info or {},
1893:                     "dest_path": dest_path,
1894:                 })
1895:             payload = {
1896:                 "paste_id": int(paste_id or 0),
1897:                 "conflicts": items,
1898:                 "has_multiple": bool(has_multiple),
1899:             }
1900:             msg = "CONFLICT_QUERY|" + json.dumps(payload)
1901:             win32file.WriteFile(pipe_handle, msg.encode("utf-8"))
1902:             win32file.CloseHandle(pipe_handle)
1903:             agent_print(f"[ClipboardAgent] CONFLICT_QUERY paste_id={paste_id} n={len(items)}")

1121: def _classify_context_menu_item(name):
1122:     """'paste' | 'not_paste' | 'unknown'."""
1123:     raw = (name or "").replace("&", "").replace("…", "").replace("...", "")
1124:     n = " ".join(raw.strip().lower().split())
1125:     if not n:
1126:         return "unknown"
1127:     compact = n.replace(" ", "").replace("+", "")
1128:     if "ctrlv" in compact:
1129:         return "paste"
1130:     first = n.split("\t", 1)[0].strip()
1131:     for tok in _PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1132:         if first == tok or first.startswith(tok + " ") or tok in first.split():
1133:             return "paste"
1134:     for tok in _NOT_PASTE_MENU_TOKENS:
1135:         if tok in n or tok.replace(" ", "") in compact:
1136:             return "not_paste"
1137:     return "unknown"

========== MODEL ==========
✅ **Build hoàn tất thành công!** 

Toàn bộ 3 thay đổi sửa lỗi của chúng ta đã được compile xong và đóng gói thành công.

| Bước | Kết quả |
|------|---------|
| 🔨 `windows_service_loop.exe` | ✅ |
| 🔨 `RemoteDesktopP2P.exe` | ✅ |
| 🔏 Ký Authenticode | ✅ |
| 📀 `EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe` | ✅ **57.2 MB** |

**Các lỗi đã được xử lý trong bản build này:**
1. **Lỗi "Give access to" (và các tính năng tương tự):** Đã bổ sung các chuỗi như "Give access to", "Send to", "Print", "Compress/Extract", "Run as administrator" (cùng các bản dịch tiếng Việt) vào danh sách loại trừ. Khi click vào các mục này ở Context Menu, Agent sẽ không còn hiểu lầm là lệnh Paste nữa.
2. **Lỗi Menu ghi đè:** Đã chuyển đổi hoàn toàn để hiển thị cửa sổ ClassicCopyDialog xác nhận ghi đè trực tiếp trên màn hình của HOST (máy bị remote) thay vì gửi sang máy client.
3. **Lỗi kẹt Clipboard cũ:** Đã thêm xử lý tự động làm sạch (clear) bộ nhớ clipboard Windows ngay lập tức khi session kết thúc (pipe bị ngắt kết nối), giúp xoá bỏ hoàn toàn hiện tượng kẹt file/dữ liệu từ phiên làm việc trước đó.

Bạn hãy lấy file cài đặt [`EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe`](file:///d:/Data/AI%20Code/remote_desktop/EasyRemoteDesktop_Installer.exe) mới nhất để cài thử nhé. File portable (`RemoteDesktopP2P.zip`) cũng đã sẵn sàng.
